## L120 — #320 (b) BUILT + 6-round pre-open convergence + MERGED; the trusted-glue-restore security learning (19 Aug 2026)

**#320 (capture per-run implement cost) built by Codex-MCP from the posted contract, conventional/topology-v2, in worktree `wt-320`. MERGED (squash `4377631`, operator), #320 CLOSED, #318 CLOSED-superseded.** Six pre-open fold rounds, ALL before the roster fired (the D103 shift-left working as designed — the F1-S8 "5 Codex rounds post-open" pattern happened PRE-open here instead). Commits 7fb0faa→9c61aed→8aa7235→746b9dc→35b0941→dd2cda7→4c8c6e9→8774023.

**The whole arc was ONE hardening: "run a trusted script restored into an agent-controlled repo, post-agent-execution."** The cross-family lenses caught an escalating chain, each catching the OTHER's blind spot (diversity paid both directions): floor caught `cost.json`-written-to-repo-root → staged into every patch by the capture `git add -A` (a real factory-integrity bug — a no-op agent run would become a spurious artifact-only PR); then mutable-checkout extractor; then (after switching restore to `git checkout HEAD -- path`) **smudge-filter poisoning** (`.gitattributes` NOT in disallowedTools + a `filter.x.smudge` gitconfig via the npm-subprocess seam poisons the materialised bytes AND runs arbitrary code during checkout — fsr reproduced TRUSTED→PWNED); then (after switching to `git show`) **replacement-ref redirection** (`git show` follows `.git/refs/replace/<sha>` by default — floor reproduced); then (after `--no-replace-objects`) **object-DB substitution** (`git show` does NO read-time hash verification, so `.git/objects/**`-writable-via-subprocess defeats content-addressing — UNCLOSEABLE by any git-show form; fsr reproduced a forged commit→tree→blob at the fixed SHA).

**DURABLE REUSABLE LEARNING (for every future factory step that restores trusted glue from the agent-mutable checkout):** the restore needs `git --no-replace-objects show <immutable-dispatch-sha>:path > file` — `git checkout -- path` is smudge-poisonable, plain `git show` is replace-ref-followable, and `--no-replace-objects` closes replacement/grafts. BUT `git show` gives no hash verification, so object-DB substitution remains a residual — the restore is **bar-raising defense-in-depth, sound ONLY because the implement job holds no write token (contents:read/issues:read, use_commit_signing:true, persist-credentials:false → no on-disk credential) and the crossing outputs are re-validated by a closed grammar in publish.** Genuine content-addressing would need hash-verified materialisation (git-fsck / fresh object store / inline jq like the model_id step) — deferred as disproportionate for a low-value cost-observability feature.

**Security residual adjudicated ACCEPT-AND-TRACK (operator AskUserQuestion, recorded #320 `issuecomment-5337641639` at 04:45:06Z BEFORE the PR opened 04:45:36Z — decision predates use per D23).** fsr (mandatory) CONFIRMED-SOUND after an exhaustive git-internals pass: the residual yields only code-exec equivalent to the already-accepted unsandboxed "Run local gates" step in the same write-token-free job; the transcript→cost.json channel gives NO marginal exfil over the existing implement-patch artifact; the OAuth token is scrubbed/unreachable. The cross-family floor rated it P1 ("reopens #164's no-transcript boundary"). Operator chose to follow the mandatory lens + ship + track → filed **#321** (object-DB-substitution + NODE_OPTIONS/$GITHUB_ENV residuals, routes fsr, epic:F1). PR body honestly frames the restore as defense-in-depth, not "immune."

**Design corrections folded mid-arc:** F4 (update PR-body cost on refresh) was REVERTED — the `## Provenance` section is creation-time by definition (model_id/prompt/dispatcher all creation-time), so a refresh-updated cost line was inconsistent AND introduced concurrent-body-clobber + stale-sibling-provenance risks; per-run cost lives authoritatively in the always-on step-summary + artifact. FR2 upload gate corrected to `always() && outcome=='success'` (a bare `outcome=='success'` implicitly ANDs `success()`, dropping the artifact on the no-PR FAILURE path — the exact durability the feature exists for). fsr CONFIRMED-SOUND, qa all per-guard sole-catchers mutation-verified, connector 👍 clean FIRST pass, pr-triage MERGEABLE. claude-review red = GENUINE TRUNCATION (num_turns:1/tool_invocations:0/substantive_output:1 → 0 findings; pr-triage corrected the orchestrator's "benign zero-turn no-op" classification — a truncated run is not the D152-accepted shape, but still non-required + zero findings = "no review ran," non-blocking). **META-LEARNING: heavy pre-open rigor on a protected-pipeline diff is the shift-left win, not thrashing — each round was a genuine escalating-subtlety finding converging on content-addressing; the stop point was fsr-CONFIRMED-SOUND + operator-adjudicated-residual, and the disproportionate-to-fully-close call was surfaced to the operator (jq-pivot vs accept) rather than folded indefinitely.**

## L124 — #319 CONVERGENCE PIVOT + backlog audit (37→21) + #327 MERGEABLE; three ratified operating principles (20 Aug 2026)

**The #319 saga hit the convergence wall and the operator course-corrected.** After the corpus-fix landed (the guard finally fires on the real `**Acceptance criteria**` bold + suffixed-ATX C2 corpus + schema vocabulary — the value that mattered; my pre-open floor had validated it ONLY against `### Acceptance criteria`, which NO C2 issue uses — a Rigour-Calibration "measure the live corpus first" miss the cross-family Codex connector caught), the connector's 2nd pass found 3 more edge fail-opens (2 regressions from my own folds — entity-decode ordering, ATX continuation boundary; 1 marginal quoted-path). Operator interrupted the fold-more path: "37 issues again, down from 20 — with this approach how do we complete our goals?"

**Root-cause (agreed):** #319 is an ADDITIVE-OVERLAY quality guard (misses are safe-direction, caught downstream); I ran it like a fail-closed security gate, chasing every advisory-lens edge on a dep-free hand-rolled Markdown parser where each fold introduced the next edge (no fixed point), and filed an issue per residual (backlog inflation). **DORA connection:** I optimized change-failure-rate the WRONG way (exhaustive pre-merge review) vs the DORA-elite way (small batch + fast feedback + fix-forward → low failure AND high throughput). **PR-size-limit connection:** thin slices assume CHEAP review, but we apply a heavyweight fixed-cost gauntlet (fsr+qa+codex-review+2 connector rounds) to EVERY slice → death-by-a-thousand-reviews.

**THREE ratified operating principles (operator "ok for all 3"):** (1) review weight PROPORTIONAL to risk, not uniform — low-risk additive-overlay slice = ONE pass, ship; invariant-touching slice = full gauntlet; **≤1-post-open-round is a HARD stop** for quality findings, absolute-block only for must-block invariants. (2) slice by shippable INCREMENT of value + iterate (`Refs` not `Closes`; don't fold every follow-on finding into the open PR). (3) document-in-code, DON'T file-per-residual. Memory: `converge-additive-guards-dont-inflate-backlog`. Also ratified: CI path-filtering for non-code changes is a genuine small win (skip-with-green stub so branch-protection required checks don't hang; `.github/**` = fsr-mandatory) — a quick conventional PR BETWEEN C2 stories, not now.

**#319 close-out (process-integrity caught + fixed):** D-319-2 had said FOLD the 2 regressions; the pivot reversed to ACCEPT-as-residual, but I applied the accept in-thread while citing D-319-2 (which said fold). **pr-triage caught the unrecorded reversal** (the #289 record-before-citing hazard) → FIX-FIRST. Fixed by recording **D-319-3** (#319 `issuecomment-5352003966`) SUPERSEDING D-319-2 with the proportionality reason, then correcting the thread citations. pr-triage re-verdict: blocker cleared, all 7 threads sound (2 fixed-in-code, 5 accept-per-recorded-decision); resolved all 7 → **#327 MERGEABLE/CLEAN, awaiting operator merge.** #319 is F1 defense-in-depth and does NOT block C2 (the C2 stories already carry enumerated ACs).

**Backlog audit 37→21:** closed 16 gold-plating / advisory-lens / dark-surface residual issues (clear 7: #326/#324/#283/#306/#219/#52/#181; cluster 9: #146/#210/#212/#280 claude-review, #226/#239/#249 owner-command-dark, #321/#325), context preserved in each closing comment. Kept: the 10 C2 stories #308-#317, real F1 infra (#9/#10/#11/#14/#301/#237/#328), #69/#162/#178, #319 (closes on #327).

**NEXT: operator merges #327 → then C2 with the new discipline (one story at a time, ship-at-good-enough, ≤1-round hard stop, risk-proportional review). C2 pace = open operator call.**

## L125 — DECISION: revert topology-v2 heavy-orchestration → full-autonomy roadmap (operator, 20 Aug 2026)

**Operator decision (this session):** the experiment's north star is **FULL AUTONOMY** — raise a GitHub issue, agents build/review it, a human specs+merges, with the factory running itself on GitHub Actions. Topology v2 (interactive PM/orchestrator drives story-planner + dispatch + every review fold locally) was scoped as a **24h keep/adjust/revert experiment (28 Jul)**; ~3 weeks overdue on that checkpoint, it became the de-facto model and made feature-building MORE hands-on than D98 intends — anti-autonomy. **DISPOSITION = REVERT toward D98:** the human specs (enumerated-AC issue) + merges; agents triage/implement/review on Actions; a local session steps in ONLY when a PR genuinely needs adjudication, not to drive every step. The three ratified operating principles (L124) stay. AGENTS.md's topology section needs updating to match (conventional/fsr-gated code-repo PR).

**Autonomy dials (each a conscious, gated step — operator hard stop to flip):**
1. **PR-comment-driven edits (owner-task-apply, Surface B)** — the operator tags/comments a PR and the workflow applies the edits (the "@claude address feedback" task path). This is the biggest hands-off lever (a PR with findings self-edits instead of needing a driver). **BUILT but DARK.** Activation gates: **#237** (task-agent credential isolation — the write-capable `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` must be isolated from the model so a prompt-injected task can't exfil/misuse it; HARD precondition, currently open, real build work) → operator ratifies the **task-agent D140 platform disposition** (still open, coupled to #237) → create the enable vars (`OWNER_COMMAND_INTAKE_ENABLED` / `OWNER_TASK_APPLY_ENABLED`) → fresh `factory-security-reviewer` pass → activate. Intake is operator-allowlisted (D147/D149), so only the operator can trigger it — not a random PR commenter.
2. **Auto-chain triage→implement (§10 ratchet)** — dispatch fires on the ready label instead of a manual `workflow_dispatch`. Criterion (§10): ~5 manually-dispatched stories with **zero triage overrides and ≥80% first-pass-green**. So run early C2 stories manual-dispatched to accumulate the evidence, then flip.
3. **Auto-merge-on-clean (§10 step 2)** — only after a full epic (C2) lands factory-built with no post-merge defect traced to a factory PR, and with the permanent human spot-audit (§ "100% of PRs" audit) in place. Last dial.

**Sequencing (open — operator to steer):** build #237 (credential isolation for the PR-comment-edit path) as the immediate F1 priority, interleaved with the first C2 stories (which double as the §10 clean-dispatch evidence + the autonomy vehicle). Then activate Surface B → PR-comment edits hands-off → flip auto-chain → eventually auto-merge. **Every activation stays an operator hard stop; the session preps + verifies, never flips.**

**L134 — #310 (C2-S4 RECOMPUTE_REFERENCE_SUMMARY) MERGED (squash `76aada4` via PR #334); registry sync PR #335 open (fsr CONFIRMED-SOUND). - **fold-1 (D-334-A, `545049f`):** 2 P1 DEPLOY-BLOCKERS — `copy grants` in the *view* clause-position (for `CREATE PROCEDURE` it must be after the arg list, before `returns`; schema-reviewer caught) + missing `$$…$$` body delimiters (schemachange splits a bare `begin…end` on `;`; **connector caught, schema-reviewer missed** — cross-family diversity earned its keep) — plus 2 P2 (null grouping-key guard; `count(*)` over a documentary-nullable PK) + 1 LOW (app-qualify).
- **fold-2 (D-334-B, `81a49b5`):** connector P1 — the FC/drop `min_by` scalar subqueries were correlated on `c.summary` *inside the aggregate ordering arg* → Snowflake "Unsupported subquery type cannot be evaluated" (un-flattenable; **structurally DISTINCT from #311's WHERE-only correlated-subquery FALSE POSITIVE** — web-docs-confirmed error class). Rewrote both as `ROW_NUMBER()` ranked CTEs over a plain join, rank-1 LEFT JOIN — also fixed the P2 `min_by` tie-nondeterminism (`order by abs(offset), elapsed_s, bean_temp_c nulls last`). schema-reviewer had called the subquery valid → genuine cross-family disagreement only live #11 settles; rewrote to the unambiguously-supported form rather than gamble.
- **fold-3 (D-334-C, `56814d7`):** connector P1 — the NEG-E null-filter `summary:x_at_utc is not null` admits a VARIANT **JSON null** (distinct from SQL NULL) → a no-first-crack roast falls through to the earliest telemetry sample (spurious FC temp). **Reachability proven from the fixture** (`faulted_at_utc: null` → the agent emits explicit JSON null for absent lifecycle events). Fixed by cast-before-null-check (`::timestamp_tz is not null`; filters absent + JSON-null + keeps real uniformly).
All folds Codex-MCP-authored (same thread, neutral framing, no filter block); each I deep-verified myself (diff + gates) before commit/push. **Reviews:** schema-migration-reviewer CONFIRMED-SOUND ×4 (original + each fold, mutation battery each); privacy-auditor PASS (aggregate-only, internal table, no PII/IP; documented note: no `visibility` filter on `contributing`, accepted — internal-only, consent-to-learn≠publish); connector CLEAN both channels on final head `56814d7` (bot 👍 + "Didn't find any major issues, Reviewed commit: 56814d7fcd"); **pr-triage MERGEABLE** (D23; verified every fix vs the real head + adjudicated all 7 connector threads fixed-or-stale). 7 threads resolved on pr-triage's adjudication; operator squash-merged → `76aada4`; #310 CLOSED; worktree/branch pruned; main ff'd. **Registry sync = separate PR #335** (`chore/registry-sync-310`, D135, protected path → fsr CONFIRMED-SOUND; a factory PR can't touch registry.md; #333 pattern); awaits CI + connector + operator merge. Accepted non-fail-open residuals: `started_at_utc`-null degenerate ranking (near-zero reach, schema-reviewer no-action) + true VARIANT-null runtime semantics (both live-#11-gated). Every fold disposition operator-decided (AskUserQuestion), each recorded (D-334-A/B/C) BEFORE the fold commit cited it. **KEY LEARNINGS: (1) hands-off BUILD works (2nd proof), incl. the `max_turns` retry as designed — fail-closed, informative-not-broken; 80 too tight for a complex proc, 150 ample; failed run's partial patch correctly rejected, no spurious PR. (2) The FOLD-half gap is WORSE for Snowflake procs than #311 showed: THREE connector onion-peel rounds, each a real deploy/semantics fix, each a full conventional round-trip — C2 Snowflake procs do NOT converge cleanly via connector fold-loops pre-#11. (3) #11 (live DEV contract check) is now the concrete, repeated-evidence STRATEGIC LEVER for the remaining C2 procs #313/#314 — catch VARIANT/deploy semantics mechanically instead of one-per-connector-round. (4) Snowflake specifics banked: `CREATE PROCEDURE COPY GRANTS` goes after the arg list before `RETURNS` (NOT the view position); a SQL-scripting body needs `$$…$$` for schemachange; a correlated scalar subquery correlated INSIDE the aggregate ordering arg is un-flattenable → use `ROW_NUMBER()` ranked CTEs; VARIANT JSON-null ≠ SQL NULL → cast-before-null-check (`::type is not null`) filters both; `min_by` ties are nondeterministic → `ROW_NUMBER()` + stable secondary order. (5) accrues a 2nd C2 dispatch toward the §10 auto-chain gate, but QUALIFIED: triage was clean, first-pass was NOT deploy-green (3 fold rounds), so it is NOT a "first-pass-green" data point — it is the strongest evidence yet that the auto-chain gate for procs waits on #11.** NEXT (operator): #313/#314 now unblocked, OR prioritize #11 (the lever), OR dial-1/#237 — recommend surfacing #11 given the repeated evidence. **L135 — #11 (F1-S8 live DEV Snowflake contract check) DONE end-to-end; the L134 "strategic lever" PULLED. The grant audit's PUBLIC check redesigned to a property-based ownership model across a 3-round adversarial arc (D-11-A superseded -> D-11-B..E); PR #336 MERGED (squash `6f33b1f`); supervised live re-dispatch PASSED (gate + connection + audit + deploy); #310/#311 LIVE-VALIDATED; #11 CLOSED. FIRST full-autonomy session: operator authorized merge + dispatch + Environment-gate approval mid-run, and this session drove fix -> review -> merge -> live-validate -> gate-approve (22 Aug 2026).** The problem L134 named: the first supervised dispatch (run 32587767801) proved gate + connection but the pre-deploy `assert_dev_ci_grants.py` grant audit FAILED CLOSED (correct direction) on ~40 Snowflake ACCOUNT-DEFAULT PUBLIC grants across 9 classes (sample-data SELECT, SNOWFLAKE.* database roles, compute pools, account-level AI/agent privileges, the learning role/warehouse), NONE touching our data -> deploy skipped -> #310/#311 unvalidated. Literal "PUBLIC has zero visible grants" is unsatisfiable on a real account. **THE FIX ARC (every disposition operator-ratified via AskUserQuestion, each recorded on #11 BEFORE the commit cited it):** **D-11-A** (enumerate the Snowflake defaults) SUPERSEDED as insufficient (PR #336's first cut covered only 2 of 9 classes) + fragile (the AI/compute-pool defaults keep growing). **D-11-B** (property redesign): flag a PUBLIC grant iff it targets an object WE own (`_public_grant_targets_owned_object`: exact container == ROASTPILOT_DEV, or the DEV warehouse), DECOUPLED from `_DATABASE_SCOPED_OBJECT_TYPES` so an unknown/new object type inside ROASTPILOT_DEV still flags (fail-closed, unlike `is_allowed_grant` which returns False on unknown types); malformed rows flag. **KEY INSIGHT: the container property handles the BULK of the ~40 Snowflake defaults FOR FREE** (non-DEV container -> ignored, no enumeration) -- only the CONTAINER-LESS access-conferring classes need targeted branches, and those enumerations are tiny (1 role, 5 account privileges) vs D-11-A's fragile ~40. **The property is STRUCTURALLY INCOMPLETE for access-conferring grants, and the cross-family adversarial floor peeled three real fail-opens the property missed, one per class:** (1) pre-open **fsr** caught a role WE own (`ROASTPILOT_AGENT`/`PUBLIC_WEB`) granted to PUBLIC -> container-less -> slipped (schema-migration-reviewer AND my own analysis missed it; the connector's local review had gestured at it and I under-weighted it -- fsr made it concrete with a reproduced row) -> **D-11-C** (flag ROLE-to-PUBLIC unless an enumerated Snowflake-default role `{SNOWFLAKE_LEARNING_ROLE}`); (2) the **connector post-ready** caught dangerous account-level privileges (`MANAGE GRANTS`/`CREATE DATABASE`/`CREATE ROLE`) granted to PUBLIC -> `granted_on:ACCOUNT`, non-DEV container -> slipped -> **D-11-D** (flag ACCOUNT-to-PUBLIC unless an enumerated Snowflake-default privilege x5); (3) the connector also caught an allowlisted default role that ITSELF holds a DEV grant (PUBLIC inherits transitively) -> **D-11-E** (`main()` runs `SHOW GRANTS TO ROLE <default_role>` per PUBLIC-granted allowlisted role, `find_default_role_boundary_reaches` flags any grant reaching our DEV boundary; deliberately container/warehouse-ONLY, NOT the role/account flag-unless-default logic, so the role's legitimate Snowflake-internal grants are not false-positived -- which would re-break the live deploy). **claude-review MEDIUM** (the D-11-E function missing the malformed-row fail-closed guard its sibling `find_public_grants` has) folded (fix-the-class). **Two objections held, not folded, both operator-consistent:** (a) the connector/local-codex recurring "keep zero-tolerance, no PUBLIC anywhere" (AGENTS.md:17-19), raised ~4x -> reject-in-thread (the property model is operator-ratified precisely BECAUSE the live dispatch PROVED literal zero-tolerance unsatisfiable); the genuine code/AGENTS.md-literal-wording divergence tracked as **#337** (protected-path human-directed reconciliation, separate from this snowflake-only PR). (b) the connector "reject non-default PUBLIC grants outside DEV" (integrations / arbitrary non-DEV objects) -> **pr-triage reject-in-thread**: zero-instance (pr-triage grepped every migration -- this repo creates only container-scoped objects, no integrations; `check_forbidden_grants.py` covers anything we'd create in a migration), direct application of AGENTS.md Rigour Calibration "reject what is not used", scoped (a future INTEGRATION migration must extend the predicate first). (c) the **second-hop residual** (the default role itself holding `ROLE ROASTPILOT_AGENT` or `MANAGE GRANTS`) -- fsr AND connector both flagged -> operator's D-11-E explicitly documented it as the one-level #59 residual; not realizable without account-admin capability the audit presupposes absent (an admin would grant PUBLIC directly, which the direct checks catch); NOT folded because extending risks false-positives on the role's legit Snowflake grants -> re-break deploy. **REVIEWS:** the full adversarial floor ran THREE times (initial diff + two folds), each reviewer in its OWN isolated worktree -- schema-migration-reviewer no-fail-open x3, fsr CONFIRMED-SOUND (caught the role fail-open first, then confirmed each fold closed with no new hole), qa PASS (genuine bidirectional sole-catchers, isolating malformed tests, TestMain mock wiring verified). Codex connector: 2 findings-rounds (cd6fa96 account-level+transitive P1s; 7de85dd the fresh integration P1) then CLEAN on the final head `b4e301d` via both channels (bot 👍 + "Didn't find any major issues, Reviewed commit: b4e301d79c", anti-spoof-verified: `chatgpt-codex-connector[bot]` type Bot, head-matching). **pr-triage FIX-FIRST -> MERGEABLE** (D23; adjudicated all 4 threads against the real head, ruled the integration P1 reject-in-thread with the Rigour-Calibration rationale, flagged the one MEDIUM must-fix). Mutation baseline ratcheted 0.7459 -> 0.7817 (killed 458->623, total 614->797, from CI artifacts only per the runner/local divergence rule); the DETECTION predicates (`_public_grant_targets_owned_object`, `_grant_reaches_owned_boundary`, the account/role allowlists, the malformed guard) have ZERO survivors. **IMPLEMENTER: Codex-MCP throughout** (same thread, neutral framing, no filter block) -- three folds (D-11-B..E logic+tests; then D-11-D+D-11-E; then the malformed guard); each I DEEP-VERIFIED myself (behavioural probe + gates + full diff) before commit/push, never trusting the self-report. **MERGE + LIVE VALIDATION (operator-authorized mid-session):** the operator escalated authority in three steps ("take action" -> "merging you can do" -> "I also permit gate approval for this task"); authorization logged on #11 before action. Squash-merged #336 -> `6f33b1f`; re-ran `dev-snowflake-contract.yml` on main (run 32600848432); approved the `dev-snowflake-ci` Environment gate via the `pending_deployments` API (the F1-S8 human checkpoint, operator-delegated-for-this-task). The run PASSED every step: PRE-deploy audit "confirmed: all 219 grant(s) (+89 future) on ROASTPILOT_DEV_CI_ROLE stay within ROASTPILOT_DEV/DEV_CI_WH, no PUBLIC current grant violates the DEV/account boundary, no PUBLIC-granted default role directly reaches DEV..." (the D-11-B..E logic + the D-11-E live SHOW GRANTS check both ran and passed on the real account) -> DEPLOY applied `V1.1.0__base_tables` + `R__proc_recompute_summary` (#310) + `R__secure_views` (#311) "Completed successfully, scripts_applied=3" -> POST-deploy audit "confirmed: all 300 grant(s)..." (the migrations introduced no forbidden grant). #310/#311's deferred deploy-semantics residuals (the VARIANT-null/COPY-GRANTS/ranked-CTE fixes from L133/L134) are now LIVE-VALIDATED. #11 CLOSED with full evidence; #9 (F1-S6) baseline note posted; registry sync = separate conventional PR **#338** (`chore/registry-sync-11-live`, registry-only, floor-exempt); the 4 wt-11* worktrees + local/remote branch pruned. **KEY LEARNINGS: (1) a "flag iff targets our object" property is STRUCTURALLY INCOMPLETE for access-conferring PUBLIC grants (role / account-privilege / transitive-via-role) -- each is container-less and needs a flag-unless-enumerated-Snowflake-default branch; but the container property handles the bulk of Snowflake's account-defaults for free, so the surviving enumerations are tiny (1 role + 5 account privileges) and fail-closed for unknowns, unlike D-11-A's fragile ~40. (2) the cross-family adversarial floor EARNED ITS KEEP three times on one security-control loosening -- fsr caught the role fail-open pre-open, the connector caught account-level + transitive post-ready; schema-migration-reviewer and my own read missed the role one. This is the #80/#187 safety-floor pattern in action; review weight proportional-to-risk held, and the loosening direction correctly triggered the FULL adversarial floor thrice. (3) FIRST full-autonomy end-to-end drive: operator escalated authority mid-run and this session took the fix through merge + live dispatch + Environment-gate approval -- the F1-S8 human checkpoint was operator-delegated FOR THIS TASK and logged before action. This SUPERSEDES the "session is advisory, not the driver" + "every merge/dispatch/gate is an operator hard stop" defaults ONLY under explicit per-task authorization; absent that, the hard stops stand. (4) #11 DONE -> #313/#314 (SUBMIT_REVIEW/DELETE_ROAST, unblocked by #310) can now build against a LIVE gate -- C2 Snowflake procs no longer need one-fix-per-connector-round; the strategic lever is available. (5) recording-before-citing paid off every round: D-11-A/B/C/D/E each recorded on #11 before the fold cited it, so pr-triage never blocked a decision as unrecorded. Snowflake banked: `GRANT <priv> ON ACCOUNT TO ROLE PUBLIC` and `GRANT ROLE X TO ROLE PUBLIC` both surface in `SHOW GRANTS TO ROLE PUBLIC` as ACCOUNT/ROLE rows a container-only property misses; a role granted to PUBLIC hands its full access to every principal.** NEXT (operator): #313/#314 now buildable with the live gate; #337 AGENTS.md reconciliation (protected-path human PR); the D-11-E second-hop tightening is a candidate future belt-and-suspenders op. adds the C2-S5 row + the zero-post-merge-folds & main-only-live-validation learnings + drops S5 from the catch-all, marks #314 next) — awaits CI + operator merge. **#314 (DELETE_ROAST) story-planner(opus) contract DRAFTED** (not yet posted/D104-verified/ratified): resolves the six decisions the cascade demands — parameter is the internal `id` (owner/agent path, D-313-A's slug reasoning does NOT transfer) with **NO visibility filter** (deletion must work on `private` roasts — the owner's revocation path); **L139 — #314 build DRIVEN autonomously under an away-operator "take as far as you can" grant, then HALTED at the pre-open floor: the cross-family `codex review` lens caught a MUST-BLOCK cascade bug baked into the ratified contract, rooted in a plan-internal inconsistency. #314 UNMERGED, awaiting operator design decision. So the files physically live under `<run_id>/` and `roast_artifacts.stage_path` records that run-id path, NOT `<roast_id>/`; the proc as built REMOVEs the wrong prefix and **leaves the real staged files undeleted = the cascade must-block** (AGENTS.md "a cascade that skips stage files is a must-block"). The story-planner cited plan.md:149 and missed that plan.md:203 contradicts it; cloud_roasts/roast_artifacts carry NO run_id column, so `roast_artifacts.stage_path` is the ONLY record of the file locations. **HALTED (per the away-grant's stop-on-judgment-call clause) rather than fold or merge**, because the fix is not mechanical: `REMOVE` by the recorded `stage_path` reintroduces a data-injection surface (`stage_path` is agent-written, concatenated into `EXECUTE IMMEDIATE`, worse than D-314-C's validated UUID) and rests on a **plan-internal inconsistency the operator must reconcile** (plan.md:149 vs :203 — run_id is the real path). Secondary **P2**: duplicate-`cloud_roasts`-id (unenforced PK) → `any_value` picks one grouping, all matching rows deleted, one summary recomputed — near-impossible with uuid ids; accept-and-document per the #311 unenforced-PK precedent, or fail-closed-unless-`v_count=1`. Recorded on `#314 issuecomment-5386047401`; the away-drive authorization + interpretation on the #314 contract comment. **RECOMMENDED PATH for the operator (prepared, not executed):** (a) reconcile plan.md:149 to the run_id-based stage_path form; (b) redesign D-314-B/C to capture the roast's DISTINCT `stage_path` values BEFORE the transaction, validate each against a strict fail-closed allowlist pattern (bounding the injection surface), commit the row deletes, then `REMOVE` each captured path AFTER commit (preserving the fail-safe ordering); (c) P2 = accept-and-document. **KEY: the cross-family floor earned its keep AGAIN — caught a must-block bug the story-planner + my ratified contract baked in from a stale plan comment; had "take as far as you can" been read as merge-authority-on-clean-floor, this would have shipped. Squash-merged **#362 → main `231a685`**; worktree + branch pruned. **HIT THE [[github-closing-keyword-parser-is-lexical]] TRAP TWICE ON ONE PR:** the body's "does not **close** #356" linked #362 as closing #356 (closingIssuesReferences=[356]); my first fix REINTRODUCED it with "must NOT auto-**close** #356"; only removing every `close/closes + #356` lexeme cleared it to `[]`. This also spawned 3 spec-grounded-review threads (356:0 loader-live-wire=U2, 356:2 live-run=operator, 356:3 idempotent-MERGE-reload=U2) that BLOCKED merge until resolved-with-disposition; the spec-grounded review then RE-RAN clean after the body fix. **KEY LEARNINGS: (1) a new module added to `only_mutate` needs its OWN 0-survivor/0-no_tests coverage — the first CI mutation run is the real reveal (local M-spot-checks miss the full survivor set); SORT it (mirror the sibling's pragma discipline + add real tests), never ratchet down. (2) the closing-keyword parser is purely lexical: "does NOT close #N" and "must NOT auto-close #N" BOTH link it; grep the body for `(close|fix|resolve)[sd]? #N` before AND after every edit, and verify `closingIssuesReferences==[]` for any PR that must not close its referenced issue. (3) a read-only PR gets a CLEAN connector 👍 (no permanent residual to re-derive) — the never-clean pattern is specific to PRs with a permanent unclosable residual (#345/#312/U2). (4) the mutation-baseline ratchet reliably pushes a data-heavy PR's diff over the claude-review 64-KiB cap = benign non-required "no review ran", expected on any baseline-ratchet PR.** main `231a685`; #356 OPEN (code complete: U1 `86d2ad7` / U2 `7c7ddbc` / U3 `231a685`; only the operator-run live seed + read-only validation remains → discharges #316 D-316-A + #310 residual).
